    Public Sub elimina_relacion_user(id_relacion As Integer)
        Try
            Dim fbcom As New FbCommand("delete from ac_act_usuarios A where a.id_relacion=@id_relacion", cnn_prod)
            fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_relacion", id_relacion)
            fbcom.ExecuteNonQuery()
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("Error: elimina_pedido_asignado_aw" & ex.Message)
        End Try
    End Sub

    Public Sub ac_guarda_substatus(orden As Integer, id_modulo As Integer, nombre_modulo As String, num_substatus As Integer, nombre_substatus As String, descripcion As String, usuario As String)
        Dim com As New FbCommand("insert into ac_substatus (orden,id_modulo,nombre_modulo,num_substatus,nombre,descipcion,usuario,fecha_alta,estatus) values (@orden,@id_modulo,@nombre_modulo,@num_substatus,@nombre,@descipcion,@usuario,@fecha_al,@estatus)", cnn_prod)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("orden", orden)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_modulo", id_modulo)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("nombre_modulo", nombre_modulo)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("num_substatus", num_substatus)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("nombre", nombre_substatus)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("descipcion", descripcion)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("usuario", usuario)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("fecha_al", Date.Now.ToString("dd.MM.yyyy HH:mm:ss"))
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("estatus", 1)
        com.ExecuteNonQuery()
    End Sub

    Public Sub ac_actualiza_substatus(id_subtarea As String, num_substatus As Integer, nombre As String, descr As String, user As String)
        Try
            Dim cmd As New FbCommand("update ac_substatus A set A.num_substatus = @num_substatus, A.nombre=@nombre, A.descipcion=@descipcion, A.usuario=@usuario where A.id_substatus = @id_substatus", cnn_prod)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("num_substatus", num_substatus)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("nombre", nombre)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("descipcion", descr)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("usuario", user)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_substatus", id_subtarea)
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox(ex.ToString, MsgBoxStyle.Critical)
        End Try
    End Sub

    Public Sub ac_elimina_substatus(id_substatus As String)
        Try
            Dim cmd As New FbCommand("update ac_substatus A set A.estatus = @estatus where A.id_substatus = @id_substatus", cnn_prod)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("estatus", 0)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_substatus", id_substatus)
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox(ex.ToString, MsgBoxStyle.Critical)
        End Try
    End Sub

    Public Sub ac_actualiza_ordem(id_subanexo As String, orden As Integer)
        Try
            Dim cmd As New FbCommand("update ac_substatus A set A.orden = @orden where A.id_substatus = @id_substatus", cnn_prod)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("orden", orden)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_substatus", id_subanexo)
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox(ex.ToString, MsgBoxStyle.Critical)
        End Try
    End Sub

    Public Function ac_activiades_usr(modulos As Integer, docto_id As Integer, id_user As Integer) As DataTable
        Try
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("WITH tbl_tareas(id_substatus,nombre,descipcion,puede,estatus_tarea, fecha, usuario_creador,list_usuarios, orden) AS (
                SELECT A.id_substatus, A.nombre, A.descipcion, IIF(COALESCE(B.id_user, 0) = 0 , 0, 1) AS puede, COALESCE(C.estatus_tarea, 'P'),COALESCE(C.fecha_modificacion, ' '),E.usuario_creador,
                COALESCE(D.nombre_usuario,COALESCE((SELECT LIST(H.nombre_usuario, ', ') FROM ac_act_usuarios G INNER JOIN usuarios H ON G.id_user = H.usuario_id WHERE IIF(E.usuario_creador = COALESCE(D.nombre_usuario,'') OR COALESCE(B.id_user, 0) <> 0 ,G.id_substatus = A.id_substatus AND G.id_user = B.id_user ,G.id_substatus = A.id_substatus)), ' ')), A.orden
                FROM ac_substatus A LEFT JOIN ac_act_usuarios B ON (A.id_substatus = B.id_substatus AND B.id_user = " & id_user & " AND A.estatus = 1)
                LEFT JOIN ac_actividades C ON (A.id_substatus = C.id_substatus AND C.id_sugerido = " & docto_id & ")
                LEFT JOIN usuarios D ON (D.usuario_id =C.id_user_act )
                LEFT JOIN doctos_sueridos E ON (E.suerido_id = " & docto_id & ")
                WHERE A.id_modulo = " & modulos & "
            )SELECT * FROM tbl_tareas ORDER BY orden", cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            If tab.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Return tab
            Else
                Return Nothing
            End If
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return Nothing
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function ac_get_notas(id_substatus As Integer, id_docto As Integer) As DataTable

    End Function

    End Sub

    'consultas de buscador
    Public Function retorna_doctos_sugeridos_pendientes_buscador(ini As Date, fin As Date, usuario_activo As String, estatus As String, buscar As String) As DataTable
        Try
            Dim sql As String = "select a.suerido_id, a.folio,a.nom_cliente,substring(a.proyecto from 1 For 60) As proyecto,b.nombre As  vendedor,a.fecha,a.usuario_creador,a.usuario_mod,a.total_proyecto,(Select z.nombre from tipos_moneda z where z.moneda_id=a.moneda_id) As moneda,a.estatus,coalesce(a.enviado_hubspot,'False') as enviado_hubspot
            From doctos_sueridos A
            inner Join vendedores B on (a.vendedor=b.vendedor_id) where a.fecha between  '" & Format(ini, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "'   and '" & Format(fin, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "' and a.estatus ='Pendiente' and (b.nombre containing '" & buscar & "' or (proyecto containing '" & buscar & "') or (a.nom_cliente containing '" & buscar & "') or (a.folio containing '" & buscar & "')) order by a.folio"
            If Len(usuario_activo) > 0 Then sql = "select a.suerido_id, a.folio,a.nom_cliente,substring(a.proyecto from 1 For 60) As proyecto,b.nombre As  vendedor,a.fecha,a.usuario_creador,a.usuario_mod,a.total_proyecto,(Select z.nombre from tipos_moneda z where z.moneda_id=a.moneda_id) As moneda,a.estatus,coalesce(a.enviado_hubspot,'False') as enviado_hubspot
            From doctos_sueridos A
            inner Join vendedores B on (a.vendedor=b.vendedor_id) where a.fecha between  '" & Format(ini, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "'   and '" & Format(fin, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "' and a.estatus ='Pendiente' and (b.nombre containing '" & buscar & "' or (proyecto containing '" & buscar & "') or (a.nom_cliente containing '" & buscar & "') or (a.folio containing '" & buscar & "')) order by a.folio"
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox(ex.Message)
            Return Nothing
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function retorna_doctos_sugeridos_facturacion_buscador(ini As Date, fin As Date, usuario_activo As String, buscar As String, folio_aw As Integer) As DataTable
        Try
            Dim sql As String = "select a.suerido_id,a.folio,a.estatus,a.nom_cliente,substring(a.proyecto from 1 for 60) as proyecto,b.nombre as  vendedor,a.fecha,a.usuario_creador,sum (c.unidades) as UDS ,a.total_proyecto,d.folio_aw
            from doctos_sueridos A
            inner join doctos_medidas_presupuesto C on (c.sugerido_id=a.suerido_id)
            inner join vendedores B on (a.vendedor=b.vendedor_id)
            left join pedidos_asignados_produccion D on (a.suerido_id = d.sugerido_id)
            where  a.estatus in ('Facturacion','Aprobado') and (b.nombre containing '" & buscar & "' or (proyecto containing '" & buscar & "') or (a.nom_cliente containing '" & buscar & "') or (a.folio containing '" & buscar & "') or d.folio_aw = '" & Convert.ToInt32(buscar) & "')
            group by a.suerido_id,a.folio,a.nom_cliente,substring(a.proyecto from 1 for 60) ,b.nombre ,a.fecha,a.usuario_creador,a.total_proyecto,a.estatus,coalesce(a.enviado_hubspot,'False'),d.folio_aw
            order by a.estatus "
            If Len(usuario_activo) > 0 Then sql = "select a.suerido_id,a.folio,a.estatus,a.nom_cliente,substring(a.proyecto from 1 for 60) as proyecto,b.nombre as  vendedor,a.fecha,a.usuario_creador,sum (c.unidades) as UDS ,a.total_proyecto
            from doctos_sueridos A
            inner join doctos_medidas_presupuesto C on (c.sugerido_id=a.suerido_id)
            inner join vendedores B on (a.vendedor=b.vendedor_id)
            left join pedidos_asignados_produccion D on (a.suerido_id = d.sugerido_id)
            where  a.estatus in ('Facturacion','Aprobado') and (b.nombre containing '" & buscar & "' or (proyecto containing '" & buscar & "') or (a.nom_cliente containing '" & buscar & "') or (a.folio containing '" & buscar & "') or d.folio_aw = '" & Convert.ToInt32(buscar) & "')
            group by a.suerido_id,a.folio,a.nom_cliente,substring(a.proyecto from 1 for 60) ,b.nombre ,a.fecha,a.usuario_creador,a.total_proyecto,a.estatus,coalesce(a.enviado_hubspot,'False'),d.folio_aw
            order by a.estatus "
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox(ex.Message)
            Return Nothing
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function retorna_doctos_sugeridos_produccion_buscador(ini As Date, fin As Date, usuario_activo As String, buscar As String, folio_aw As Integer) As DataTable
        Try
            Dim sql As String = "select a.suerido_id,a.folio,a.estatus,a.nom_cliente,substring(a.proyecto from 1 for 60) as proyecto,b.nombre as  vendedor,a.fecha,a.usuario_creador,sum (c.unidades) as UDS ,a.total_proyecto, d.folio_aw
            ,(select t.FOLIOS_PEDIDOS from retorna_folios_pedidos_prod2 (a.suerido_id) t) as PEDIDOS_AW
            from doctos_sueridos A
            inner join doctos_medidas_presupuesto C on (c.sugerido_id=a.suerido_id)
            inner join vendedores B on (a.vendedor=b.vendedor_id) 
            left join pedidos_asignados_produccion D on (a.suerido_id = d.sugerido_id)
            where a.estatus ='Produccion' and (b.nombre containing '" & buscar & "' or (proyecto containing '" & buscar & "') or (a.nom_cliente containing '" & buscar & "') or (a.folio containing '" & buscar & "') or d.folio_aw = '" & folio_aw & "')
            group by a.suerido_id,a.folio,a.nom_cliente,substring(a.proyecto from 1 for 60) ,b.nombre ,a.fecha,a.usuario_creador,a.total_proyecto,a.estatus,coalesce(a.enviado_hubspot,'False'), d.folio_aw
            order by a.folio "
            If Len(usuario_activo) > 0 Then sql = "select a.suerido_id,a.folio,a.estatus,a.nom_cliente,substring(a.proyecto from 1 for 60) as proyecto,b.nombre as  vendedor,a.fecha,a.usuario_creador,sum (c.unidades) as UDS ,a.total_proyecto, d.folio_aw
            ,(select t.FOLIOS_PEDIDOS from retorna_folios_pedidos_prod2 (a.suerido_id) t) as PEDIDOS_AW
            from doctos_sueridos A
            inner join doctos_medidas_presupuesto C on (c.sugerido_id=a.suerido_id)
            inner join vendedores B on (a.vendedor=b.vendedor_id) 
            left join pedidos_asignados_produccion D on (a.suerido_id = d.sugerido_id)
            where a.estatus ='Produccion' and (b.nombre containing '" & buscar & "' or (proyecto containing '" & buscar & "') or (a.nom_cliente containing '" & buscar & "') or (a.folio containing '" & buscar & "' or d.folio_aw = '" & folio_aw & "'))
            group by a.suerido_id,a.folio,a.nom_cliente,substring(a.proyecto from 1 for 60) ,b.nombre ,a.fecha,a.usuario_creador,a.total_proyecto,a.estatus,coalesce(a.enviado_hubspot,'False'), d.folio_aw
            order by a.folio "
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox(ex.Message)
            Return Nothing
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function retorna_doctos_sugeridos_pendientes_entregados_buscador(ini As Date, fin As Date, usuario_activo As String, estatus As String, buscar As String, folio_aw As Integer) As DataTable
        Try
            Dim sql As String = "select a.suerido_id,a.folio,a.nom_cliente,substring(a.proyecto from 1 for 60) as proyecto,b.nombre as  vendedor,a.fecha,a.usuario_creador,a.total_proyecto,
            c.nombre as moneda,a.estatus,
            a.empresa_envio,a.fecha_envio_ped,
            d.folio_aw
            from doctos_sueridos A
            inner join tipos_moneda C on (a.moneda_id=c.moneda_id)
            inner join vendedores B on (a.vendedor=b.vendedor_id) 
            left join pedidos_asignados_produccion D on (a.suerido_id = d.sugerido_id)
            where a.fecha between '" & Format(ini, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "'   and '" & Format(fin, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "' and a.estatus ='" & estatus & "' and (b.nombre containing '" & buscar & "' or (proyecto containing '" & buscar & "') or (a.nom_cliente containing '" & buscar & "') or (a.folio containing '" & buscar & "') or (d.folio_aw = '" & Convert.ToInt32(buscar) & "')) order by a.folio "
            If Len(usuario_activo) > 0 Then sql = "select a.suerido_id,a.folio,a.nom_cliente,substring(a.proyecto from 1 for 60) as proyecto,b.nombre as  vendedor,a.fecha,a.usuario_creador,a.total_proyecto,
            c.nombre as moneda,a.estatus,
            a.empresa_envio,a.fecha_envio_ped,
            d.folio_aw
            from doctos_sueridos A
            inner join tipos_moneda C on (a.moneda_id=c.moneda_id)
            inner join vendedores B on (a.vendedor=b.vendedor_id)
            left join pedidos_asignados_produccion D on (a.suerido_id = d.sugerido_id)
            where a.fecha between '" & Format(ini, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "'   and '" & Format(fin, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "' and a.usuario_creador='" & usuario_activo & "' and a.estatus ='" & estatus & "' and (b.nombre containing '" & buscar & "' or (proyecto containing '" & buscar & "') or (a.nom_cliente containing '" & buscar & "') or (a.folio containing '" & buscar & "') or (d.folio_aw = '" & Convert.ToInt32(buscar) & "')) order by a.folio "
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox(ex.Message)
            Return Nothing
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function retorna_comision(suerido_id As Integer) As Double
        Try
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("select coalesce(comision_vendedor,0) AS comision from doctos_sueridos where suerido_id = '" & suerido_id & "'", cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            If tab.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Return tab.Rows(0).Item(0)
            Else
                Return 1
            End If
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return 1
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function retorna_Id_QB(id_suerido As Integer)

            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            If tab.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Return tab.Rows(0).Item(0)
            Else
                Return 1
            End If
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return 1
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function incrementa_invoice_id(invoice As Integer)
        Try
            Dim com As New FbCommand("update or insert into invoice_qb (id,invoice_id) values (@id,@invoice_id)", cnn_prod)
            com.Parameters.AddWithValue("id", 1)
            com.Parameters.AddWithValue("invoice_id", invoice)
            com.ExecuteNonQuery()

        End Try
        Return 0
    End Function

    Public Function guarda_docto_rack(id_sugerido As Integer, grid As DataGridView)
        Try
            For Each x As DataGridViewRow In grid.Rows

                If x.Cells(11).Value = "0" Then

                    Dim fbcom As New FbCommand("update or insert into doctos_racks (folio,id_sugerido,id_tipo_rack,peso_maximo,operador,estatus,fecha_inicio,fecha_fin,disponible,piezas,peso)
                     values (@folio,@id_sugerido,@id_tipo_rack,@peso_maximo,@operador,@estatus,@fecha_inicio,@fecha_fin,@disponible,@piezas,@peso) matching(folio,id_sugerido)", cnn_prod)
                    fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("folio", x.Cells(0).Value)
                    fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_sugerido", id_sugerido)
                    fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_tipo_rack", x.Cells(1).Value)
                    fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("peso_maximo", x.Cells(3).Value)
                    fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("operador", x.Cells(4).Value)
                    fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("estatus", x.Cells(5).Value)
                    fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("fecha_inicio", x.Cells(6).Value)
                    fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("fecha_fin", x.Cells(7).Value)
                    fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("disponible", x.Cells(8).Value)
                    fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("piezas", x.Cells(9).Value)
                    fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("peso", x.Cells(10).Value)
                    fbcom.ExecuteNonQuery()
                Else
                    Dim fbcom As New FbCommand("update or insert into rel_rack_final (id_sugerido,id_sugerido_rack) values (@id_sugerido,@id_sugerido_rack) matching(id_sugerido,id_sugerido_rack)", cnn_prod)
                    fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_sugerido", id_sugerido)
                    fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_sugerido_rack", x.Cells(1).Value)
                    fbcom.ExecuteNonQuery()
                End If
            Next
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("error al guardar" & ex.Message)
        End Try
        Return 0
    End Function

    Public Function eliminar_renglon_grid_racks(id_sugerido As Integer, folio As Integer)
        Try
            Dim fbcom As New FbCommand("delete from doctos_racks where id_sugerido =@id_sugerido and folio = @folio", cnn_prod)
            fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_sugerido", id_sugerido)
            fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("folio", folio)
            fbcom.ExecuteNonQuery()
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("error al eliminar el registro del reack: " & folio & "error: " & ex.Message)
        End Try
        Return 0
    End Function
    Public Function retorna_doctos_rack(id_sugerido As Integer)
        Try
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("select a.folio, a.id_tipo_rack, b.nombre, peso_maximo, coalesce(a.operador,'sin asignar') as operador,a.estatus,a.fecha_inicio,a.fecha_fin,a.disponible,coalesce(a.piezas,0),coalesce(a.peso,0),iif(coalesce(C.id_sugerido_rack,0) = 0,0,1) as tipo_rel_rack
                from doctos_racks A
                inner Join tipo_rack B on (id_tipo_rack = tipo_rack)
                left join rel_rack_final C on (A.folio = C.id_sugerido_rack)
                where a.id_sugerido = " & id_sugerido & "order by a.folio", cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return Nothing
        End Try
    End Function
    Public Function retorna_doctos_rack2(id_sugerido As Integer)
        Try
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("select a.folio, a.id_tipo_rack,B.nombre, peso_maximo, coalesce(a.operador,'sin asignar') as operador,a.estatus,a.fecha_inicio,a.fecha_fin,a.disponible,coalesce(a.piezas,0),coalesce(a.peso,0),iif(A.id_sugerido = C.id_sugerido, 0,1) as tipo_rel_rack, C.id_sugerido_rack, A.ancho, A.alto, A.largo
                from rel_rack_final C
                inner join doctos_racks A on (A.sugerido_rack = C.id_sugerido_rack)
                inner join tipo_rack B on (A.id_tipo_rack = B.tipo_rack)
                where C.id_sugerido = " & id_sugerido & " order by a.folio", cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return Nothing
        End Try
    End Function
    Public Sub actualiza_entregado_sugeridos(sugerido_id As Integer, estatus As String)
        Try
            Dim fbcom As New FbCommand("update doctos_sueridos A set a.estatus=@estatus
            where a.suerido_id =@docto_sug_id", cnn_prod)
            fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("docto_sug_id", sugerido_id)
            fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("estatus", estatus)
            fbcom.ExecuteNonQuery()
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("Error: actualiza_entregado_sugeridos " & ex.Message)
        End Try
    End Sub

    Public Sub eliminar_racks_envios_sugerido(sugerido_id As String, rack_sugerido_id As String)
        Try
            Dim fbcom As New FbCommand("delete from racks_envios_sugerido where rack_sugerido_id = @rack_sugerido_id and sugerido_id = @sugerido_id", cnn_prod)
            fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("sugerido_id", sugerido_id)
            fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("rack_sugerido_id", rack_sugerido_id)
            fbcom.ExecuteNonQuery()
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("Error: error al intentar borrar el rack: " & ex.Message)
        End Try
    End Sub
    Public Function ret_doc_su_pen_totales(ini As Date, fin As Date, usuario_activo As String, estatus As String) As DataTable
        Try
            Dim sql As String = "SELECT A.folio, A.total_proyecto, sum(iif( A.moneda_id = 2, coalesce((B.total_pies_cuadrados * B.costo_compra),0), coalesce((B.total_mts_cuadrados * B.costo_compra),0))) as costo_materiales,
                A.sistema_dim_id, A.moneda_id, coalesce(A.costo_flete,0), coalesce(A.costo_embalaje,0),coalesce(A.comision_vendedor,0), coalesce( A.status_factoraje, 0),
                sum(coalesce((D.total*B.total_mts_cuadrados),0))
                from doctos_sueridos A inner join doctos_medidas_presupuesto B on (A.suerido_id = B.sugerido_id)
                inner join vendedores C on (a.vendedor=C.vendedor_id)
                left join costos_adicionales_presupuestos D on (d.sugerido_id = A.sugerido_id and d.costo_adicional_id = 1224 and d.sugerido_id <> 0) where a.fecha between  '" & Format(ini, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "'   and '" & Format(fin, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "' and a.estatus ='" & estatus & "'group by A.folio, A.total_proyecto, A.sistema_dim_id, A.moneda_id, A.costo_flete, A.costo_embalaje,A.comision_vendedor,A.status_factoraje "
            If Len(usuario_activo) > 0 Then sql = "select A.folio, A.total_proyecto, sum(iif( A.moneda_id = 2, coalesce((B.total_pies_cuadrados * B.costo_compra),0), coalesce((B.total_mts_cuadrados * B.costo_compra),0))) as costo_materiales,
                A.sistema_dim_id, A.moneda_id, coalesce(A.costo_flete,0), coalesce(A.costo_embalaje,0),coalesce(A.comision_vendedor,0), coalesce( A.status_factoraje, 0),
                sum(coalesce((D.total*B.total_mts_cuadrados),0))
                from doctos_sueridos A inner join doctos_medidas_presupuesto B on (A.suerido_id = B.sugerido_id)
                inner join vendedores C on (a.vendedor=C.vendedor_id)
                left join costos_adicionales_presupuestos D on (d.sugerido_id = A.sugerido_id and d.costo_adicional_id = 1224 and d.sugerido_id <> 0) where a.fecha between '" & Format(ini, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "'   and '" & Format(fin, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "' and a.usuario_creador='" & usuario_activo & "' and a.estatus ='" & estatus & "' group by A.folio, A.total_proyecto, A.sistema_dim_id, A.moneda_id, A.costo_flete, A.costo_embalaje,A.comision_vendedor,A.status_factoraje "

            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox(ex.Message)
            Return Nothing
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function ret_doc_su_fact_tot(ini As Date, fin As Date, usuario_activo As String) As DataTable
        Try
            Dim sql As String = "select A.folio, A.total_proyecto, sum(iif( A.moneda_id = 2, coalesce((B.total_pies_cuadrados * B.costo_compra),0), coalesce((B.total_mts_cuadrados * B.costo_compra),0))) as costo_materiales,
                A.sistema_dim_id, A.moneda_id, coalesce(A.costo_flete,0), coalesce(A.costo_embalaje,0),coalesce(A.comision_vendedor,0), coalesce( A.status_factoraje, 0),
                sum(coalesce((D.total*B.total_mts_cuadrados),0))
                from doctos_sueridos A inner join doctos_medidas_presupuesto B on (A.suerido_id = B.sugerido_id)
                inner join vendedores C on (a.vendedor=C.vendedor_id)
                left join costos_adicionales_presupuestos D on (d.sugerido_id = A.sugerido_id and d.costo_adicional_id = 1224 and d.sugerido_id <> 0 )
                where a.estatus in ('Facturacion','Aprobado')
                group by A.folio, A.total_proyecto, A.sistema_dim_id, A.moneda_id, A.costo_flete, A.costo_embalaje,A.comision_vendedor,A.status_factoraje"
            If Len(usuario_activo) > 0 Then sql = "select A.folio, A.total_proyecto, sum(iif( A.moneda_id = 2, coalesce((B.total_pies_cuadrados * B.costo_compra),0), coalesce((B.total_mts_cuadrados * B.costo_compra),0))) as costo_materiales,
                A.sistema_dim_id, A.moneda_id, coalesce(A.costo_flete,0), coalesce(A.costo_embalaje,0),coalesce(A.comision_vendedor,0), coalesce( A.status_factoraje, 0),
                sum(coalesce((D.total*B.total_mts_cuadrados),0))
                from doctos_sueridos A inner join doctos_medidas_presupuesto B on (A.suerido_id = B.sugerido_id)
                inner join vendedores C on (a.vendedor=C.vendedor_id)
                left join costos_adicionales_presupuestos D on (d.sugerido_id = A.sugerido_id and d.costo_adicional_id = 1224 and d.sugerido_id <> 0 )
                where a.usuario_creador='" & usuario_activo & "' and  a.estatus in ('Facturacion','Aprobado')
                group by A.folio, A.total_proyecto, A.sistema_dim_id, A.moneda_id, A.costo_flete, A.costo_embalaje,A.comision_vendedor,A.status_factoraje"
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox(ex.Message)
            Return Nothing
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function ret_do_su_pend_ent_tot(ini As Date, fin As Date, usuario_activo As String, estatus As String, estatus2 As String) As DataTable
        Try
            Dim sql As String = "select A.folio, A.total_proyecto, sum(iif( A.moneda_id = 2, coalesce((B.total_pies_cuadrados * B.costo_compra),0), coalesce((B.total_mts_cuadrados * B.costo_compra),0))) as costo_materiales,
                A.sistema_dim_id, A.moneda_id, coalesce(A.costo_flete,0), coalesce(A.costo_embalaje,0),coalesce(A.comision_vendedor,0), coalesce( A.status_factoraje, 0),
                sum(coalesce((D.total*B.total_mts_cuadrados),0))
                from doctos_sueridos A inner join doctos_medidas_presupuesto B on (A.suerido_id = B.sugerido_id)
                inner join vendedores C on (a.vendedor=C.vendedor_id)
                left join costos_adicionales_presupuestos D on (d.sugerido_id = A.sugerido_id and d.costo_adicional_id = 1224 and d.sugerido_id <> 0 )
                where a.fecha between '" & Format(ini, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "'   and '" & Format(fin, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "' and (a.estatus ='" & estatus & "' or a.estatus ='" & estatus2 & "')
                group by A.folio, A.total_proyecto, A.sistema_dim_id, A.moneda_id, A.costo_flete, A.costo_embalaje,A.comision_vendedor,A.status_factoraje"
            If Len(usuario_activo) > 0 Then sql = "select A.folio, A.total_proyecto, sum(iif( A.moneda_id = 2, coalesce((B.total_pies_cuadrados * B.costo_compra),0), coalesce((B.total_mts_cuadrados * B.costo_compra),0))) as costo_materiales,
                A.sistema_dim_id, A.moneda_id, coalesce(A.costo_flete,0), coalesce(A.costo_embalaje,0),coalesce(A.comision_vendedor,0), coalesce( A.status_factoraje, 0),
                sum(coalesce((D.total*B.total_mts_cuadrados),0))
                from doctos_sueridos A inner join doctos_medidas_presupuesto B on (A.suerido_id = B.sugerido_id)
                inner join vendedores C on (a.vendedor=C.vendedor_id)
                left join costos_adicionales_presupuestos D on (d.sugerido_id = A.sugerido_id and d.costo_adicional_id = 1224 and d.sugerido_id <> 0 )
                where a.fecha between '" & Format(ini, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "'   and '" & Format(fin, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "' and a.usuario_creador='" & usuario_activo & "' and (a.estatus ='" & estatus & "' or a.estatus ='" & estatus2 & "')
                group by A.folio, A.total_proyecto, A.sistema_dim_id, A.moneda_id, A.costo_flete, A.costo_embalaje,A.comision_vendedor,A.status_factoraje"
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox(ex.Message)
            Return Nothing
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Sub actualiza_notas_opt(sugerido_id As Integer, notas As String, Optional n_user As String = "")
        Try
            Dim fbcom As New FbCommand("update cp_notas_opt A set a.notas=@nota,a.usuario=@user where a.id_sugerido=@docto_sug_id", cnn_prod)
            fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("docto_sug_id", sugerido_id)
            fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("nota", notas)
            fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("user", n_user)
            fbcom.ExecuteNonQuery()
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("Error: actualiza " & ex.Message)
        End Try
    End Sub
    Public Sub cp_guarda_nota_opt(id_sugerido As Integer, notas As String, n_user As String)
        Dim com As New FbCommand("insert into cp_notas_opt (id_sugerido,notas, usuario) values (@id_sugerido,@notas, @usuario)", cnn_prod)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_sugerido", id_sugerido)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("notas", notas)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("usuario", n_user)
        com.ExecuteNonQuery()
    End Sub
    ''' <summary>
    '''  cambiar esta para el total del acumulado  el cambio se hace de us_costo y fecha costo a us_confirma y fecha confirma 
    ''' </summary>
    ''' <param name="id_sugerido"></param>
    ''' <param name="costo"></param>
    ''' <returns></returns>
    Public Function guarda_costo_confirmacion(ByVal id_sugerido As Integer, ByVal costo As Double)
        Try
            Dim com As New FbCommand("update or insert into doctos_confirma (id_sugerido,total_costo,us_confirma,fecha_confirma) values (@id_sugerido,@total_costo,@us_confirma,@fecha_confirma) matching (id_sugerido) ", cnn_prod)
            com.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_sugerido", id_sugerido)
            com.Parameters.AddWithValue("total_costo", costo)
            com.Parameters.AddWithValue("us_confirma", usr_gl)
            com.Parameters.AddWithValue("fecha_confirma", Now())
            com.ExecuteNonQuery()
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("Error alta de costo sugerido " & ex.Message)
        End Try
        Return 0
    End Function

    Public Function guarda_costo_cero(ByVal id_sugerido As Integer, ByVal costo As Double)

            com.Parameters.AddWithValue("total_costo", costo)

            com.Parameters.AddWithValue("fecha_costo", Now())
            com.ExecuteNonQuery()
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("Error alta de costo sugerido " & ex.Message)
        End Try
        Return 0
    End Function

    Public Function gat_costo_to_confirm(id_sugerido As Integer) As Double
        Try
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("select coalesce(total_costo,0) from doctos_confirma  where  id_sugerido = '" & id_sugerido & "'", cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            If tab.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Return tab.Rows(0).Item(0)
            Else
                Return 0
            End If
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return 0
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function gat_costo_confirmed(id_sugerido As Integer) As Integer
        Try
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("select iif(coalesce(us_confirma,'-') = '-',0,1) as confirma from doctos_confirma  where  id_sugerido = '" & id_sugerido & "'", cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            If tab.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Return tab.Rows(0).Item(0)
            Else
                Return 0
            End If
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return 0
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function guarda_costoSugAW(ByVal id_sugerido As Integer, ByVal folio As Integer, id_suerido As Integer, tipo As Integer)
        Try
            Dim com As New FbCommand("update or insert into doctos_confirma_det (id_sug,us_costo,fecha_costo,folio,id_suerido,tipo) values (@id_sug,@us_costo,@fecha_costo,@folio,@id_suerido,@tipo) matching (id_suerido,tipo) ", cnn_prod)
            com.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_sug", id_sugerido)
            com.Parameters.AddWithValue("us_costo", usr_gl)
            com.Parameters.AddWithValue("fecha_costo", Now())
            com.Parameters.AddWithValue("folio", folio)
            com.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_suerido", id_suerido)
            com.Parameters.AddWithValue("tipo", tipo)
            com.ExecuteNonQuery()
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("Error alta de folios AW " & ex.Message)
        End Try
        Return 0
    End Function

    Public Function get_permisos_confirmar(id_usrio As Integer) As DataTable
        Try
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("SELECT coalesce(B.valor, 'F'),A.descripcion FROM permisos_usuarios A
                LEFT JOIN permisos_asignados_usuarios B ON (A.permiso_id = B.permiso_id AND usuario_id = '" & id_usrio & "')
                WHERE A.permiso_id IN (30170,30171) ORDER BY A.orden", cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            If tab.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Return tab
            Else
                Return New DataTable
            End If
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return New DataTable
        End Try
    End Function
    Public Function get_listaCrstales_Favoritos(id_usuario As Integer, id_sugerido As Integer) As DataTable
        Try
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("with cris_prove(cristal_id,descripcion,costo,tipo_cambio,nombre,id_provedor ) as (
                Select B.cristal_id, B.descripcion || ' DE ' || B.espesor || 'mm',B.costo,C.tipo_cambio,C.nombre,coalesce(B.id_proveedor, 999) as id_proveedor from rel_cris_fav A
                inner Join costos_cristales_presupuestos B on (A.id_cristal = B.cristal_id )
                inner join tipos_moneda C on (B.moneda_id=C.moneda_id)
                where(A.id_user = '" & id_usuario & "' Or A.id_user = '48')
                union
                select A.cristal_id, B.descripcion || ' DE ' || B.espesor || 'mm',B.costo,C.tipo_cambio,C.nombre,coalesce(B.id_proveedor, 999) as id_proveedor from sug_venta_laminado_det A
                inner join costos_cristales_presupuestos B on (A.cristal_id = B.cristal_id)
                inner join tipos_moneda C on (B.moneda_id=C.moneda_id)
                where sugerido_id = '" & id_sugerido & "'
                ) select cristal_id,descripcion,costo,tipo_cambio,nombre from cris_prove order by id_provedor", cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            If tab.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Return tab
            Else
                Return New DataTable
            End If
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox(ex.ToString)
            Return New DataTable
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function retorna_provedores_crsitales() As DataTable
        Try
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("select id_provedor, provedor from proveedores where activo = 1 order by id_provedor", cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            If tab.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Return tab
            Else
                Return New DataTable

    Public Function retorna_info_crsitales() As DataTable
        Try
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("select a.cristal_id,a.descripcion || ' DE ' || a.espesor || 'mm' as cristal,c.provedor as proveedor,D.sustrato,a.espesor,a.costo,b.tipo_cambio,b.nombre, coalesce(a.id_proveedor, 999) as id_provedor,coalesce(a.id_sustrato, 99) as id_sustrato, coalesce(E.id_user, 0),coalesce(a.is_proyecto, 0) as is_proy
                from costos_cristales_presupuestos A inner join tipos_moneda B on (a.moneda_id=b.moneda_id)
                left join proveedores C on (coalesce(a.id_proveedor, 999) = C.id_provedor)
                left join cristal_sustrato D on (a.id_sustrato = D.id_sustrato)
                left join rel_cris_fav E on (a.cristal_id = E.id_cristal and E.id_user = '" & id_usurio & "')
                where a.activo = 0
                order by a.descripcion , a.espesor", cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            If tab.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Return tab
            Else
                Return New DataTable
            End If
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return New DataTable
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function retorna_total_fav() As Integer
        Try
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("select count(id_cristal) from rel_cris_fav where id_user = " & id_usurio, cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            If tab.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Return Integer.Parse(tab.Rows(0).Item(0).ToString())
            Else
                Return 0
            End If
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return 0
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function consulata_cristales_proyectos() As DataTable
        Dim tab As New DataTable
        Try
            'Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("select a.id_proyecto,a.nombre_proyecto,a.nombre_proyecto_real,a.configuracion, a.cantidad, b.descripcion, a.medida_h || ' X ' || a.medida_w as medida
            '    from proyectos_cristales A
            '    inner join costos_cristales_presupuestos B on (A.id_cristal = B.cristal_id )", cnn_prod)
            Dim sql_q As String = String.Format("select a.id_proyecto,a.nombre_proyecto,a.nombre_proyecto_real,a.configuracion, a.cantidad, coalesce((select list(b.descripcion) from costos_cristales_presupuestos B where B.is_proyecto = A.id_proyecto and B.activo = 0),'') as descripcion, a.medida_h || ' X ' || a.medida_w as medida,coalesce(a.notas, '') as Observaciones
                from proyectos_cristales A")
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql_q, cnn_prod)
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
        Catch ex As Exception
        End Try
        Return tab
    End Function

    Public Function pc_guarda_proyecto_c(nom_proy As String, nom_proy_r As String, confi As String, ubicacion As String, id_estado As Integer, cantidad As String, f_ini As Date, f_fin As Date, id_crital As Integer, id_coating As Integer, m_h As Integer, m_w As Integer, aplicacion As String, f_entrga As Date, estatus As Integer, ubicacion_r As String, notas As String) As Integer
        Dim tab_id As New DataTable
        Dim st_id As String = "select id_elemnt from GENERA_ID_GENERAL"
        Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(st_id, cnn_prod)
        fbdat.Fill(tab_id)
        Dim id_proy As Integer = tab_id.Rows(0).Item(0)
        Dim com As New FbCommand("insert into proyectos_cristales (id_proyecto,nombre_proyecto,nombre_proyecto_real,configuracion,ubicacion,id_estado,cantidad, fecha_inicio,fecha_fin,id_cristal,id_coating,medida_h, medida_w,aplicacion,fecha_entrega,id_us_agrega,fecha_add,estatus,ubicacion_real,notas) values (@id_proyecto,@nombre_proyecto,@nombre_proyecto_real,@configuracion,@ubicacion,@id_estado,@cantidad,@fecha_inicio,@fecha_fin,@id_cristal,@id_coating,@medida_h,@medida_w,@aplicacion,@fecha_entrega,@id_us_agrega,@fecha_add,@estatus,@ubicacion_real,@notas)", cnn_prod)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_proyecto", id_proy)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("nombre_proyecto", nom_proy)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("nombre_proyecto_real", nom_proy_r)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("configuracion", confi)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("ubicacion", ubicacion)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_estado", id_estado)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("cantidad", cantidad)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("fecha_inicio", f_ini)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("fecha_fin", f_fin)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_cristal", id_crital)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_coating", id_coating)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("medida_h", m_h)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("medida_w", m_w)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("aplicacion", aplicacion)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("fecha_entrega", f_entrga)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_us_agrega", id_usurio)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("fecha_add", Now())
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("estatus", estatus)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("ubicacion_real", ubicacion_r)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("notas", notas)
        com.ExecuteNonQuery()
        Return id_proy
    End Function
    ''' <summary>
    ''' recordar obtener primero el id del cristal
    ''' </summary>
    Public Function guarda_cristales_nuevos(descripcion As String, espesor As Integer, costo As Double, id_moneda As Integer, desc_ingles As String, id_provedor As Integer, id_sustrato As Integer, is_proy As Integer) As Integer
        Dim tab_id As New DataTable
        Dim st_id As String = "select ID_CRISTAL from get_id_cristal"
        Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(st_id, cnn_prod)
        fbdat.Fill(tab_id)
        Dim id_cristal As Integer = tab_id.Rows(0).Item(0)
        Dim com As New FbCommand("insert into costos_cristales_presupuestos (cristal_id,descripcion,espesor,costo,moneda_id,descripcion_ingles,id_proveedor,id_sustrato,is_proyecto,activo) values (@cristal_id,@descripcion,@espesor,@costo,@moneda_id,@descripcion_ingles,@id_proveedor,@id_sustrato,@is_proyecto,@activo)", cnn_prod)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("cristal_id", id_cristal)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("descripcion", descripcion)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("espesor", espesor)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("costo", costo)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("moneda_id", id_moneda)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("descripcion_ingles", desc_ingles)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_proveedor", id_provedor)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_sustrato", id_sustrato)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("is_proyecto", is_proy)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("activo", 0)
        com.ExecuteNonQuery()
        Return id_cristal
    End Function

    Public Function retorna_racks_disponibles(ByVal id_sug As Integer) As DataTable
        Dim tab As New DataTable
        Try
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("select A.folio,A.sugerido_rack,A.id_tipo_rack,B.nombre,A.peso_maximo,coalesce(A.operador,'sin asignar') as operador,A.estatus,A.fecha_inicio,A.fecha_fin,A.disponible,A.piezas,A.peso from doctos_racks A inner join tipo_rack B on (A.id_tipo_rack = B.tipo_rack) where A.disponible = 'Si' and A.id_sugerido <> '" & id_sug & "'", cnn_prod)

    Public Function retorna_folios_relacion(ByVal id_rack As Integer, ByVal is_sugerido As Integer) As DataTable
        Dim tab As New DataTable
        Try
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("select list(distinct(B.folio)) as folios from rel_rack_final A
                inner join doctos_sueridos B on (B.suerido_id = A.id_sugerido)
                where A.id_sugerido_rack = " & id_rack & " and B.suerido_id <> " & is_sugerido, cnn_prod)
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
        Catch ex As Exception
        End Try

    End Function
    Public Function elimina_rel_rack(id_rack As Integer, id_sugerido As Integer)

            fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_sugerido", id_sugerido)
            fbcom.ExecuteNonQuery()

        End Try
        Return 0
    End Function
    Public Function retorna_datos_credito(ByVal is_sugerido As Integer) As DataTable
        Dim tab As New DataTable
        Try
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("select B.total from doctos_medidas_presupuesto A
                inner join costos_adicionales_presupuestos B on (posicion_det = B.sugerido_id and B.costo_adicional_id = '34091')
                where A.sugerido_id = '" & is_sugerido & "'
                group by B.total", cnn_prod)
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
        Catch ex As Exception
        End Try
        Return tab
    End Function

            fbdat.Fill(tab)
        Catch ex As Exception
        End Try
        Return tab
    End Function

    Public Function retorna_us_cotizadores() As DataTable
        Dim tab As New DataTable
        Try
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("select usuario_id,nombre_usuario,id_hubspot from usuarios where ((id_hubspot is not null) or ( id_hubspot > 0 )) and estatus = 'A'", cnn_prod)
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
        Catch ex As Exception
        End Try
        Return tab
    End Function

    Public Function guarda_actualiza_cristal(id_cristal As Integer, descripcion As String, espesor As Integer, costo As Double, id_moneda As Integer, desc_ingles As String, id_provedor As Integer, id_sustrato As Integer, is_proy As Integer, activo As Integer) As Integer
        Dim id_cristal_l As Integer = id_cristal
        If id_cristal = 0 Then
            Dim tab_id As New DataTable
            Dim st_id As String = "select ID_CRISTAL from get_id_cristal"
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(st_id, cnn_prod)
            fbdat.Fill(tab_id)
            id_cristal_l = tab_id.Rows(0).Item(0)
        End If

        If id_cristal_l = 0 Then Throw New System.NullReferenceException("El id del cristal es 0")

        Dim com As New FbCommand("update or insert into costos_cristales_presupuestos (cristal_id,descripcion,espesor,costo,moneda_id,descripcion_ingles,id_proveedor,id_sustrato,is_proyecto,activo) values 
            (@cristal_id,@descripcion,@espesor,@costo,@moneda_id,@descripcion_ingles,@id_proveedor,@id_sustrato,@is_proyecto,@activo) matching (cristal_id)", cnn_prod)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("cristal_id", id_cristal_l)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("descripcion", descripcion)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("espesor", espesor)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("costo", costo)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("moneda_id", id_moneda)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("descripcion_ingles", desc_ingles)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_proveedor", id_provedor)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_sustrato", id_sustrato)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("is_proyecto", is_proy)
        com.Parameters.AddWithValue("activo", activo)
        com.ExecuteNonQuery()
        Return id_cristal_l
    End Function
    Public Function guarda_racks_temp(id As Integer, docto_id As Integer, cantidad As Integer, largo As Double, alto As Double, ancho As Double) As Integer

        Dim id_rack_aux As Integer = id
        If id = 0 Then
            Dim tab_id As New DataTable
            Dim st_id As String = "select ID_ELEMNT from genera_id_general"
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(st_id, cnn_prod)
            fbdat.Fill(tab_id)
            id_rack_aux = tab_id.Rows(0).Item(0)
        End If

        Dim fbcom As New FbCommand("update or insert into tem_racks_eg (id_tem_rak,id_sugerido,cantidad,largo,ancho,alto,estatus)
            values (@id_tem_rak,@id_sugerido,@cantidad,@largo,@ancho,@alto,@estatus) matching (id_tem_rak,id_sugerido)", cnn_prod)
        fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_tem_rak", id_rack_aux)
        fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_sugerido", docto_id)
        fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("cantidad", cantidad)
        fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("largo", largo)
        fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("ancho", alto)
        fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("alto", ancho)
        fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("estatus", 1)
        fbcom.ExecuteNonQuery()
        Return id_rack_aux
    End Function

    Public Function retorna_archivos_compartidoas(ByVal id_sugerido As Integer, ByVal identif As Integer) As DataTable
        Dim tab As New DataTable
        Try
            Dim query As String = String.Format("select A.archivo_id, A.nombre_archivo, B.id_sugerido from archivos_adjuntos A
                inner join archivos_combinados_inf B on (A.archivo_id = B.id_archivo and A.identificador = B.tipo_archivo)
                where B.id_sugerido = {0} and B.tipo_archivo = {1}
                order by A.archivo_id", id_sugerido, identif)
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(query, cnn_prod)
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
        Catch ex As Exception
        End Try
        Return tab
    End Function

        End Try

    End Function

    Public Function Consulta_info_to_Trnslate(ByVal id_pos As Integer) As DataTable
        Dim tab As New DataTable
        Try
            Dim query As String = String.Format("select B.laminado,C.cristal_id, C.descripcion || ' Espesor ' || C.espesor as descripcion,coalesce(B.cos_templa_pos,0) as templado,coalesce(B.cos_saques_pos,0) as saques,coalesce(B.PROCESO_IMP_DIG_SECUN_ID,'') AS proceso_imp_dig,
                coalesce(B.cos_impdig_pos,0) as imprecion,coalesce(B.cos_esmerilado,0) as esmerilado,coalesce(B.cos_canto_pos,0) as canto,coalesce(B.COS_FM_POS,0) as filoM,coalesce(B.relleno_id,0) as relleno_id,
                coalesce(D.descripcion, '') as na_rell,coalesce(D.es_perfil,'N') AS ES_PERFIL
                from sug_venta_laminado_det B
                inner join costos_cristales_presupuestos c ON (B.cristal_id=C.cristal_id)
                left join rellenos D on (D.relleno_id = B.relleno_id AND NOT D.relleno_id IN(999999,61749))
                where B.sugerido_id={0} order by B.sugerido_id_det ", id_pos)
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(query, cnn_prod)
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
        Catch ex As Exception
        End Try
        Return tab
    End Function
    Public Function Consulta_TO_Traduccion(ByVal id_pos As Integer) As DataTable
        Dim tab As New DataTable
        Try
            Dim query As String = String.Format("select B.laminado,C.cristal_id, coalesce(C.descripcion, '') as descripcion, C.espesor ,coalesce(B.cos_templa_pos,0) as templado,coalesce(B.cos_saques_pos,0) as saques,coalesce(B.PROCESO_IMP_DIG_SECUN_ID,'') AS proceso_imp_dig,
                coalesce(B.cos_impdig_pos,0) as imprecion,coalesce(B.cos_esmerilado,0) as esmerilado,coalesce(B.cos_canto_pos,0) as canto,coalesce(B.COS_FM_POS,0) as filoM,coalesce(B.relleno_id,0) as relleno_id,
                coalesce(D.descripcion, '') as na_rell, coalesce(D.espesor, 0) as espesor,B.capas ,coalesce(D.es_perfil,'N') AS ES_PERFIL, B.piezas
                from sug_venta_laminado_det B
                inner join costos_cristales_presupuestos c ON (B.cristal_id=C.cristal_id)
                left join rellenos D on (D.relleno_id = B.relleno_id AND NOT D.relleno_id IN(999999,61749))
                where B.sugerido_id={0} order by B.sugerido_id_det ", id_pos)
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(query, cnn_prod)
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
        Catch ex As Exception
        End Try
        Return tab
    End Function

                coalesce(D.descripcion_ingles, '') as na_rell,coalesce(D.es_perfil,'N') AS ES_PERFIL
                from sug_venta_laminado_det B
                inner join costos_cristales_presupuestos c ON (B.cristal_id=C.cristal_id)
                left join rellenos D on (D.relleno_id = B.relleno_id AND NOT D.relleno_id IN(999999,61749))
                where B.sugerido_id={0} order by B.sugerido_id_det ", id_pos)
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(query, cnn_prod)
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
        Catch ex As Exception
        End Try
        Return tab
    End Function
    Public Function retorna_if_habilitadocreditos(folio As Integer) As Integer

            If tab.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                Return tab.Rows(0).Item(0)
            Else

            End If
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return 0
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function retorna_proyecto_comision(fecha_ini As Date, fecha_fin As Date, estatus As String)
        Try
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("select a.suerido_id,a.folio,a.proyecto,coalesce(a.costo_flete,0) as Presupuesto_Flete,coalesce(a.costo_embalaje,0) as Presupuesto_Embalaje,coalesce(a.comision_vendedor,0) As Presupuesto_Comision,a.total_proyecto,coalesce(a.total_credito,0) as Presupuesto_Credito,b.nombre,b.moneda_id,coalesce(a.status_factoraje,0) as factoraje from doctos_sueridos A
inner join clientes B on (a.no_cliente=b.cliente_id)
            where a.fecha between '" & Format(fecha_ini, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "'and '" & Format(fecha_fin, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "' and a.estatus ='" & estatus & "'", cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return Nothing
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function retorna_costo_materiales(id_sugerido As Integer, moneda As Integer)
        Try
            Dim sql As String
            If moneda = 2 Then
                sql = "select sum(tot_m2 * costo_compra) as total from(
                        select x.posicion_det,sum(total_pies_cuadrados) as tot_m2,x.costo_compra from(
                            select a.posicion_det,a.total_pies_cuadrados,a.costo_compra from doctos_medidas_presupuesto A where a.sugerido_id ='" & id_sugerido & "'
                        ) x group by  x.posicion_det,x.costo_compra  order by x.posicion_det desc)"
            Else
                sql = "select sum(tot_m2 * costo_compra) as total from(
                        select x.posicion_det,sum(total_mts_cuadrados) as tot_m2,costo_compra from(
                            select a.posicion_det,a.unidades,a.total_mts_cuadrados,a.costo_compra from doctos_medidas_presupuesto A where a.sugerido_id ='" & id_sugerido & "'
                        ) x group by  x.posicion_det,x.costo_compra  order by x.posicion_det desc)"
            End If
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return Nothing
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function retorna_comisiones(id_comisionista As Integer)
        Try
            Dim sql As String
            sql = "select a.* from comisiones_tipo A
                    inner join rel_comisiones B on (b.id_comision = a.id_comision)
                    where b.id_comisionista = '" & id_comisionista & "'"
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("error al cargar comisionitas")
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function retorna_pedidos_comision(folio As Integer)
        Try
            Dim sql As String
            sql = "select d.folio_aw,d.sugerido_id,c.proyecto from doctos_sueridos C
                    inner join (select a.folio_aw,a.sugerido_id from pedidos_asignados_produccion A
                    left join doctos_pago_comisiones_det B on (a.folio_aw=b.descripcion and b.id_tipo_comision=2001)
                    where b.descripcion is Null and a.sugerido_id = (select suerido_id from doctos_sueridos where folio = '" & folio & "') order by folio_aw
                    ) D on (d.sugerido_id = c.suerido_id)"
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("Error al obtener los pedidos no pagados" & ex.Message)
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function guarda_docto_pago(id_pago As Integer, folio As Integer, id_comisionista As Integer, monto As Double, descripcion As String, grid_pagos_posiciones As DataGridView)
        Try
            If id_pago = 0 Then
                Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("select * from gen_id_pago", cnn_prod)
                Dim tab As New DataTable
                fbdat.Fill(tab)
                id_pago = tab.Rows(0).Item(0)
                Dim fbdat2 As New FbDataAdapter("select * from gen_folio_pago", cnn_prod)
                Dim tab2 As New DataTable
                fbdat2.Fill(tab2)
                folio = tab2.Rows(0).Item(0)
                'MsgBox(id_pago & " . " & folio)
            End If
            Dim fbcom As New FbCommand("update or insert into doctos_pago_comisiones (id_pago,folio,id_comisionista,monto,descripcion,usuario_creador)
                     values (@id_pago,@folio,@id_comisionista,@monto,@descripcion,@usuario_creador) matching(folio,id_pago)", cnn_prod)
            fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_pago", id_pago)
            fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("folio", folio)
            fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_comisionista", id_comisionista)
            fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("monto", monto)
            fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("descripcion", descripcion)
            fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("usuario_creador", usr_gl)
            fbcom.ExecuteNonQuery()
            guarda_docto_pago_det(id_pago, grid_pagos_posiciones)
            Return folio
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("Error al guardar el pago: " & ex.Message)
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Sub guarda_docto_pago_det(id_pago As Integer, grid_pagos_posiciones_det As DataGridView)
        Try
            Dim monto As Double = 0
            For Each x As DataGridViewRow In grid_pagos_posiciones_det.Rows
                Dim porUt As Double = 0
                Try
                    If x.Cells(1).Value = 2004 Then
                        Dim por As Double = CDbl(x.Cells(5).Value.ToString.Replace("%", ""))
                        If (IsNumeric(por)) Then
                            If por >= 0 Then
                                porUt = por
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                Catch ex As Exception
                End Try
                Dim fbcom As New FbCommand("update or insert into doctos_pago_comisiones_det (id_pago,id_sugerido,id_tipo_comision,descripcion,monto,tipo_cambio,porcentaje)
                     values (@id_pago,@id_sugerido,@id_tipo_comision,@descripcion,@monto,@tipo_cambio,@porcentaje) matching(id_pago,descripcion)", cnn_prod)
                fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_pago", id_pago)
                fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_sugerido", x.Cells(0).Value)
                fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_tipo_comision", x.Cells(1).Value)
                fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("descripcion", x.Cells(2).Value)
                If (IsNumeric(x.Cells(3).Value)) Then monto = x.Cells(3).Value
                fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("monto", monto)
                fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("tipo_cambio", x.Cells(4).Value)
                fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("porcentaje", porUt)
                fbcom.ExecuteNonQuery()
            Next
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("Error: guarda_medidas_documento " & ex.Message)
        End Try
    End Sub

    Public Function retorna_presupuesto_flete(id_costo As Integer, folio As Integer) As DataTable
        Try
            ErrMSG = "Hay eroro de doble fbAdapter. fill ---> despues de revicion no hay error porque en todos los usos de esta tabla solo se ocupa la primera row y aunque se duplique solo se toma encunta la priema"
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("select * from importes_presupuestos(" & id_costo & "," & folio & ")", cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            If tab.Rows.Count > 0 Then
                fbdat.Fill(tab)
                Return tab
            End If
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return Nothing
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Sub guarda_precio_flete_real(f_docto_cm_id As Integer, f_compra As String, f_factura As String, f_docto_cp_id As Integer, grid_prorrateo As DataGridView, user As String)
        Try
            For Each x As DataGridViewRow In grid_prorrateo.Rows
                Dim fbcom As New FbCommand("update or insert into precio_real_flete(id_precio_flete,id_sugerido,monto_pesos,monto,racks,docto_cm_id,folio_compra,folio_factura,docto_cp_id,usuario)
                values (@id_precio_flete,@id_sugerido,@monto_pesos,@monto,@racks,@docto_cm_id,@folio_compra,@folio_factura,@docto_cp_id,@usuario) matching(id_sugerido,docto_cm_id,docto_cp_id)", cnn_prod)
                fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_precio_flete", x.Cells(7).Value)
                fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_sugerido", x.Cells(0).Value)
                fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("monto_pesos", x.Cells(5).Value)
                fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("monto", x.Cells(6).Value)
                fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("racks", x.Cells(2).Value)
                fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("docto_cm_id", f_docto_cm_id)
                fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("folio_compra", f_compra)
                fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("folio_factura", f_factura)
                fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("docto_cp_id", f_docto_cp_id)
                fbcom.Parameters.AddWithValue("usuario", user)
                fbcom.ExecuteNonQuery()
            Next
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("Error: No se guardo el costo." & ex.Message)
        End Try
    End Sub

    Public Sub CTZguardaProvedores(ByRef id_provedor As Integer, ByVal nombre As String, ByVal direcicon As String, ByVal notas As String)
        Try
            If id_provedor = 0 Then
                Dim tab_id As New DataTable
                Dim st_id As String = "select id_elemnt from GENERA_ID_GENERAL"
                Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(st_id, cnn_prod)
                fbdat.Fill(tab_id)
                id_provedor = tab_id.Rows(0).Item(0)
            End If

            Dim cmd As New FbCommand("update or insert into proveedores_cotiza (id_provee,nombre,direccion,notas) values (@id_provee,@nombre,@direccion,@notas) matching (id_provee)", cnn_prod)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_provee", id_provedor)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("nombre", nombre)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("direccion", direcicon)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("notas", notas)
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox(ex.ToString, MsgBoxStyle.Critical)
        End Try
    End Sub

    Public Function retorna_facturas_usada(cm_id As Integer, cp_id As Integer, id As Integer)
        'id=1 viene de flete y buscara en precio_flete_real id=2 viene de embalaje y buscara en precio_real_embalaje
        Dim sql As String = ""
        If (id = 1) Then
            sql = "select coalesce(count(docto_cm_id),0) from precio_real_flete where docto_cm_id = " & cm_id & " and docto_cp_id= " & cp_id
        ElseIf (id = 2) Then
            sql = "select coalesce(count(docto_cm_id),0) from precio_real_embalaje where docto_cm_id = " & cm_id & " and docto_cp_id= " & cp_id
        End If
        Try
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab.Rows(0).Item(0)
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return Nothing
        End Try
    End Function
    Public Function GetFacturasUsadasEnvios(cm_id As Integer, cpid As Integer) As Integer
        Try
            Dim sql As String = "select sum(total) as TotalF from (
                select coalesce(count(docto_cm_id),0) as total  from precio_real_flete where docto_cm_id =" & cm_id & " and docto_cp_id=" & cpid & "
                union
                select coalesce(count(docto_cm),0) as total from env_facturas where docto_cm =" & cm_id & " and docto_cp = " & cpid & ") as facturas"
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return Integer.Parse(tab.Rows(0).Item(0))
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return 1
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function retorna_proyectos_comision(folio As Integer)
        Try
            Dim sql As String
            sql = "select  a.suerido_id,a.folio,a.estatus,a.moneda_id,iif((select id_pago_det from doctos_pago_comisiones_det where descripcion = a.folio and id_tipo_comision = 2004)>1,1,0) from doctos_sueridos A
                   where a.folio =" & folio
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("Error al obtener los proyectos no pagados")
        End Try
    End Function
    Public Function retorna_montos_comisiones(suerido As Integer)
        Try
            Dim sql As String
            sql = "select a.id_pago_det,a.id_tipo_comision,a.descripcion,a.monto,a.tipo_cambio, b.folio,c.nombre,(select nombre from comisiones_tipo where id_comision = a.id_tipo_comision) as tipo_comision from doctos_pago_comisiones_det A
                    inner join doctos_pago_comisiones B on (b.id_pago = a.id_pago)
                    inner join comisionistas C on (c.id_comisionista = b.id_comisionista)
                    where a.id_sugerido ='" & suerido & "'"
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("error al obtener monto de comisiones.")
        End Try
    End Function
    Public Function retorna_proyectos_externo(folio As Integer)
        Try
            Dim sql As String
            sql = "select  a.suerido_id,a.folio,a.estatus,a.moneda_id,coalesce(comision_vendedor,0),iif((select id_pago_det from doctos_pago_comisiones_det where descripcion = a.folio and id_tipo_comision = 2008)>1,1,0),a.tipo_cambio from doctos_sueridos A
                   where a.folio =" & folio
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("Error al obtener los proyectos no pagados.")
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Sub CTZguardacotizacion(ByRef id_cot As Integer, ByVal id_sug As Integer, ByVal folio As Integer, ByVal id_prov As Integer, ByVal nsmePrve As String, ByVal precio As Double, ByVal fe_in As String, ByVal fe_ent As String, ByVal icterm As String, ByVal fe_ve As String, ByVal embala As String, ByVal flet As String, ByVal activa As Integer, ByVal id_moneda As Integer, ByVal nombre As String, ByVal tc As Double, ByVal nota As String)
        Try
            If id_cot = 0 Then
                Dim tab_id As New DataTable
                Dim st_id As String = "select id_elemnt from GENERA_ID_GENERAL"
                Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(st_id, cnn_prod)
                fbdat.Fill(tab_id)
                id_cot = tab_id.Rows(0).Item(0)
            End If

            Dim cmd As New FbCommand("update or insert into cotizacionesext (id_cotizacion,id_sugerido,folio_sugerido, id_proveedor, name_provedor, precio, fecha_entrega, fecha_cotizacion, icoterm, id_usuario, fecha_r, cotizacionactiva,fecha_vence, embalaje, flete,id_serie, moneda, tc, notas) values (@id_cotizacion,@id_sugerido,@folio_sugerido, @id_proveedor, @name_provedor, @precio, @fecha_entrega, @fecha_cotizacion, @icoterm, @id_usuario, @fecha_r, @cotizacionactiva,@fecha_vence, @embalaje, @flete,@id_serie,@moneda,@tc, @notas) matching (id_cotizacion)", cnn_prod)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_cotizacion", id_cot)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_sugerido", id_sug)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("folio_sugerido", folio)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_proveedor", id_prov)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("name_provedor", nsmePrve)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("precio", precio)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("fecha_entrega", fe_in)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("fecha_cotizacion", fe_ent)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("icoterm", icterm)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_usuario", id_usurio)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("fecha_r", Now())
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("cotizacionactiva", activa)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("fecha_vence", fe_ve)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("embalaje", embala)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("flete", flet)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("id_serie", id_moneda)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("moneda", nombre)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("tc", tc)
            cmd.Parameters.AddWithValue("notas", nota)
            'id_serie, moneda, tc
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox(ex.ToString, MsgBoxStyle.Critical)
        End Try
    End Sub
    Public Function retorna_CotizacionesExt(ByVal f1 As Date, ByVal f2 As Date) As DataTable
        Dim sql As String = "select a.suerido_id, a.folio,a.nom_cliente,substring(a.proyecto from 1 for 60) as proyecto,b.nombre as vendedor,a.usuario_creador,a.usuario_mod,a.total_proyecto
            from  cotizacionesext C
            inner join doctos_sueridos A on (A.suerido_id = C.id_sugerido)
            inner join vendedores B on (a.vendedor=b.vendedor_id)
            where A.fecha between '" & Format(f1, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "' and '" & Format(f2, "dd.MM.yyyy") & "' 
            group by a.suerido_id, a.folio,a.nom_cliente, proyecto,b.nombre,a.usuario_creador,a.usuario_mod,a.total_proyecto
            order by a.folio"

        Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
        Dim tab As New DataTable
        fbdat.Fill(tab)
        Return tab
    End Function
    Public Function retorna_CotizacionesExtFolio(ByVal id_sug As Integer) As DataTable

        Dim sql As String = "select id_cotizacion,folio_sugerido, id_proveedor, name_provedor, precio, fecha_entrega, fecha_cotizacion, icoterm, cotizacionactiva, fecha_vence, embalaje, flete, id_serie, moneda, tc, notas
            from cotizacionesext
            where id_sugerido = " & id_sug & "
            union
            select 1, folio, 1, 'MEGALUMINIO', total_proyecto,fecha, fecha,'No Aplica', 0, vigencia, costo_embalaje, costo_flete,moneda_id,iif(moneda_id = 1,'MXN','USD'),iif(moneda_id = 1,1,tipo_cambio), notas
            from doctos_sueridos where suerido_id = " & id_sug

        Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
        Dim tab As New DataTable
        fbdat.Fill(tab)
        Return tab
    End Function
    Public Function retornaPosicionesFolio(ByVal id_sug As Integer) As DataTable
        Dim sql As String = "select C.posicion_det, C.posicion, C.unidades, C.ancho_mm, C.alto_mm, C.refer_pos from doctos_sueridos B
            inner join doctos_medidas_presupuesto C on (B.suerido_id = C.sugerido_id and B.estatus = 'Pendiente' )
            where B.suerido_id = " & id_sug & "  and C.posicion_det <> 0 order by C.posicion"
        Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
        Dim tab As New DataTable

            inner join doctos_pago_comisiones B on (b.id_pago = a.id_pago)
            where a.descripcion = '" & fb & "' and b.id_comisionista = '" & comisionista & "'"

            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab

        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function retorna_proyectos_no_pagados(fecha_ini As Date, fecha_fin As Date)
        Try
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter("select a.suerido_id,a.folio,a.proyecto,e.nombre,coalesce(a.costo_embalaje,0) as Presupuesto_Embalaje,coalesce(a.comision_vendedor,0) As Presupuesto_Comision,a.total_proyecto,coalesce(a.total_credito,0) as Presupuesto_Credito,d.nombre,d.moneda_id,coalesce(a.total_credito,0) as Presupuesto_Credito from doctos_sueridos A
                                            inner join clientes D on (d.cliente_id = a.no_cliente)
                                            inner join tipos_sugeridos C on (c.id_sugerido = a.tipo_sugerido)
                                            inner join vendedores E on (e.vendedor_id = a.vendedor)
                                            left join doctos_pago_comisiones_det B on (a.folio = b.descripcion and b.id_tipo_comision = 2004)
                                            where b.descripcion is Null and a.estatus = 'Entregado'  and a.folio > 1085 and c.gra_comicion = 1 order by folio", cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return Nothing
        End Try
    End Function
    Public Function retorna_comisionistas_embalaje()

                   where b.id_comision = 2003"

    End Function
    Public Function retorna_rcks_no_pagados(sugerido As Integer, pagado As Integer)
        Try
            Dim sql As String = ""
            If (pagado = 0) Then sql = "select folio,id_tipo_rack,id_sugerido,ancho,largo,alto from doctos_racks where id_sugerido ='" & sugerido & "' and estatus not in ('requiere atencion')  and piezas > 0 and pagado = '" & pagado & "'"
            If (pagado = 1) Then sql = "select folio,id_tipo_rack,id_sugerido,ancho,largo,alto from doctos_racks where id_sugerido ='" & sugerido & "' and estatus='Finalizado'  and piezas > 0 and pagado = '" & pagado & "'"
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("Error al retornar racks no pagados")
            Return Nothing
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function retorna_ListaPedidos() As DataTable
        Try
            Dim sql As String = "select  A.proyecto, A.folio, sum(B.tot_piezas) as Piezas,list(B.folio_aw) as pedidos from doctos_sueridos A
                inner join pedidos_asignados_produccion B on (A.suerido_id = B.sugerido_id)
                where A.estatus ='Produccion'
                group by A.proyecto, A.folio"
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
            Dim tab As New DataTable
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            MsgBox("Error al retornar racks no pagados")
            Return Nothing
        End Try
    End Function

    Public Function retornaTareasSubstatus(id_sug As Integer)
        Dim tab = New DataTable
        Try
            Dim sql = "select count(C.estatus_tarea) as completas,count(A.id_substatus) as tareas
                FROM ac_substatus A
                LEFT JOIN ac_actividades C ON (A.id_substatus = C.id_substatus AND C.id_sugerido = " & id_sug & " AND C.estatus_tarea = 'C')
                WHERE A.id_modulo = 111 AND A.estatus = 1"
            Dim fbdat As New FbDataAdapter(sql, cnn_prod)
            fbdat.Fill(tab)
            Return tab
        Catch ex As Exception
            Return tab
        End Try
    End Function
